# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-09-21)

## Corpus Check
- 2990 files · ~8,994,393 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 46633 nodes · 117595 edges · 2148 communities (1154 shown, 994 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2743 edges (avg confidence: 0.67)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b4a16e64`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
